  GROUP_NAME:
  - C2xTh
  GROUP_ORDER:
  - 2^16*3^10*5^3*7^2*13*19*31
  LAST_LINE: []
  MINIMAL_NORM:
  - either 10 or 12
  NAME:
  - Thompson-Smith
  NOTES:
  - Even unimodular lattice with minimum
  - ' >= 10 (Lempken, Schroeder, Tiep (preprint))'
  - and <= 12 (explicit vector of norm 12)
  REFERENCES:
  - J. G. Thompson, A simple subgroup of E_8(3), in
  - Finite Groups Symposium, ed. N. Iwahori, Japan Soc. Promotion Science, Tokyo, 1976, pp. 113-116.
  - J. G. Thompson, Finite groups and even lattices, J. Alg.  38 (1976), 523-524.
  - J. G. Thompson, A conjugacy theorem for E_8$, J. Alg. 38 (1976), 525--530.
  - P. E. Smith, A simple subgroup of M? and E_8(3), BLMS 8 (1976), 161--165.
  - Originally thought by J. G. Thompson to have minimal norm 16;
  - this error is repeated in SPLAG, 3rd edition, page xlvi.
  - A. I. Kostrikin and P. H. Tiep, Orthogonal Decompositions and Integral Lattices,
  - De Gruyter, Berlin, 1994.
  - Lempken, Schroeder, Tiep, preprint, 2000.
